If we use Sinon to wait for that\n  // timeout to trigger, then that\'ll play back one move... but it will\n  // still leave various promises unresolved. `handleMove` won\'t return,\n  // which means `handleRedo` won\'t return, which means the next replay\n  // timeout won\'t be created. This means we can\'t just wait for any\n  // amount of time... we need create another promise which will resolve\n  // along with those other unresolved promises...\n  const advanceReplaySteps = async (n: number) => {\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      const promise = new Promise(resolve => {\n        setTimeout(resolve, replayTimeout)\n      })\n\n      // ...then `await` THAT\n      await promise\n    }\n  }\n\n  it(\'lets you replay a too-long replay\', async () => {\n    const warn = sinon.stub(console, \'warn\')\n\n    const game = renderGame()\n\n    // Replay keeps pressing Down after game over\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\n    ])\n    prompt.restore()\n\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay.\n    await advanceReplaySteps(300)\n\n    assert.deepEqual(warn.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Ignoring input replay step because mode is\', \'GAME_OVER\']\n    ])\n\n    game.unmount()\n    warn.restore()\n  })\n\n  it(\'lets you keep playing after an incomplete replay runs out of moves\', async () => {\n    const game = renderGame()\n\n    // Replay is a single move which does not end the game\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\n    prompt.returns(\'D\')\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\n    ])\n    prompt.restore()\n\n    // Play back the one move of the supplied replay\n    await advanceReplaySteps(1)\n\n    // The replay is finished but the game isn\'t over. This means we can\'t just wait for any\n  // amount of time... we need create another promise which will resolve\n  // along with those other unresolved promises...\n  const advanceReplaySteps = async (n: number) => {\n    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n      const promise = new Promise(resolve => {\n        setTimeout(resolve, replayTimeout)\n      })\n\n      // ...then `await` THAT\n      await promise\n    }\n  }\n\n  it(\'lets you replay a too-long replay\', async () => {\n    const warn = sinon.stub(console, \'warn\')\n\n    const game = renderGame()\n\n    // Replay keeps pressing Down after game over\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\n    ])\n    prompt.restore()\n\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay.\n    await advanceReplaySteps(300)\n\n    assert.deepEqual(warn.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Ignoring input replay step because mode is\', \'GAME_OVER\']\n    ])\n\n    game.unmount()\n    warn.restore()\n  })\n\n  it(\'lets you keep playing after an incomplete replay runs out of moves\', async () => {\n    const game = renderGame()\n\n    // Replay is a single move which does not end the game\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\n    prompt.returns(\'D\')\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\n    ])\n    prompt.restore()\n\n    // Play back the one move of the supplied replay\n    await advanceReplaySteps(1)\n\n    // The replay is finished but the game isn\'t over. The player must\n    // not be stuck on the "replaying..." screen: the move buttons must\n    // be back, and moves must work.\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'down\').length, 1)\n\n    await user.keyboard(\'{Down}\')\n    // The first piece is an S: after the replay\'s D plus this one,\n    // two of its cells are on the bar\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'well__cell well__cell--live\').length, 2)\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'well__cell well__cell--bar well__cell--live\').length, 2)\n    game.unmount()\n  })\n\n  it(\'lets you undo and stops replaying when a replay is in progress\', async () => {\n    const game = renderGame()\n\n    // Replay is an incomplete game\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\n    ])\n    prompt.restore()\n\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay\n    await advanceReplaySteps(60)\n\n    await user.keyboard(\'{Control>}z{/Control}\')\n    // TODO: assert that `wellStateId` is now decremented?\n    game.unmount()\n  })\n})\n'}, 'isError': False, 'timestamp': 1787274141751}

The player must\n    // not be stuck on the "replaying..." screen: the move buttons must\n    // be back, and moves must work.\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'down\').length, 1)\n\n    await user.keyboard(\'{Down}\')\n    // The first piece is an S: after the replay\'s D plus this one,\n    // two of its cells are on the bar\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'well__cell well__cell--live\').length, 2)\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'well__cell well__cell--bar well__cell--live\').length, 2)\n\n    // The game loop keeps working past the end of the replay: the\n    // piece can still be dropped (15 more moves to the floor) and\n    // locked (the 16th Down), and a new piece is dealt in PLAYING mode\n    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {\n      await user.keyboard(\'{Down}\')\n    }\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'well__cell well__cell--landed\').length, 4)\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'well__cell well__cell--live\').length, 4)\n    assert.equal(screen.queryAllByTestId(\'down\').length, 1)\n    game.unmount()\n  })\n\n  it(\'lets you undo and stops replaying when a replay is in progress\', async () => {\n    const game = renderGame()\n\n    // Replay is an incomplete game\n    const prompt = sinon.stub(window, \'prompt\')\n    prompt.returns(\'AAAA AAAA AAAA\')\n    await user.click(screen.getByTestId(\'replay-button\'))\n    assert.deepEqual(prompt.getCalls().map(call => call.args), [\n      [\'Paste replay string...\']\n    ])\n    prompt.restore()\n\n    // Play beyond the end of the supplied replay\n    await advanceReplaySteps(60)\n\n    await user.keyboard(\'{Control>}z{/Control}\')\n    // TODO: assert that `wellStateId` is now decremented?\n    game.unmount()\n  })\n})\n'}, 'isError': False, 'timestamp': 1787275019796}
